{"lang_code":"en","pos":"prep_phrase","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The product is duly prohibited by law, but in any event, it is still available on the black market.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["At any rate: anyhow, regardless (emphasizing that a point doesn't matter or hardly matters)."],"synonyms":[{"word":"at all events"},{"word":"in any case"},{"word":"anyway"},{"word":"nonetheless"},{"word":"nevertheless"},{"word":"come what may"}],"tags":["idiomatic"]},{"examples":[{"text":"You can grumble all you like, but in any event, you'd better be there at the appointed time.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["At any rate: by any measure, any way one slices it, in every case, no matter what (emphasizing that a point matters so much that there is no case in which it does not matter)."],"synonyms":[{"word":"at all events"},{"word":"in any case"},{"word":"no matter what"}],"tags":["idiomatic"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Kinetics might be described as a series of events, and in any event, there is some instant at which some change begins.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used other than figuratively or idiomatically: see in, any, event."]}],"upstream_line":1971271,"upstream_offset":6282048743,"upstream_record_sha256":"6260b71a232f40f08d1fc202359d118e807adcdbeecce2fa289edd0bfb5c75f8","word":"in any event"}
